\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left[\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{3}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right)\left[\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{3}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{4}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{3}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{3}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{3}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right),

which gives

\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal G}_{i,xx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})(X_{i}^{-1})^{2}_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}=\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\frac{\delta^{2}_{0}}{t^{3/2}}+\frac{\delta^{2}_{0}}{t^{2}}\right).

(2.53)

From the definition of
\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{i}
we have

\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\mathcal{H}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}=\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right)=\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}}{t}.

Then we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal H}_{i}\widetilde{w}_{i,xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+2\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal H}_{i,x}\widetilde{w}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal H}_{i,xx}\widetilde{w}_{i,x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal H}_{i}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+2\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal H}_{i,x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal H}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}}{\sqrt{t}}\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{t^{3/2}}\right)+3\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\mathcal{H}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}}{\sqrt{t}}\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{t^{3/2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}}{t}\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}}{\sqrt{t}}\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{t^{3/2}}\right).

(2.54)

Furthermore, we estimate

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal G}_{i,x}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})(X_{i}^{-1})_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\mathcal{G}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\frac{\delta_{0}^{3}}{t^{3/2}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{3}}{t^{2}}\right),

(2.55)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xxx}X_{i,t}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|X_{i,t}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{t^{3/2}}\right)\left(\delta_{0}+\frac{\delta_{0}}{\sqrt{t}}\right),

(2.56)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xx}X_{i,tx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})(X_{i}^{-1})_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|X_{i,tx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|(X_{i}^{-1})_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|X_{i,tx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{t}\right)\frac{\delta_{0}}{\sqrt{t}},

(2.57)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,x}X_{i,txx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})(X_{i}^{-1})^{2}_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|X_{i,txx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|(X_{i}^{-1})^{2}_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|X_{i,txx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}}{t}\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}}{t^{2}},

(2.58)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,x}X_{i,tx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})(X_{i}^{-1})_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|X_{i,tx}(\cdot,X_{i}^{-1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|(X_{i}^{-1})_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}})\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{t^{2}}.

(2.59)

We also have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|{\cal A}_{i,xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\bigg(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{x}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{xx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u-u^{*}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\widetilde{w}_{xxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left(\delta_{0}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{xxxx}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{t^{3/2}}\right).

(2.60)

From (
2.52
), (
2.53
), (
2.54
), (
2.55
), (
2.56
), (
2.57
), (
2.58
), (
2.59
), and (
2.60
) we get for
\displaystyle C_{7}>0
large enough

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|u_{i,xxx}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{C_{3}\delta_{0}}{2t^{3/2}}+C_{7}\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{t-s}}\left(\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{s^{2}}+\frac{\delta_{0}^{2}}{s^{3/2}}\right)ds\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{C_{3}\delta_{0}}{2t^{3/2}}+\frac{4\sqrt{2}C_{7}\delta_{0}^{2}}{t^{3/2}}+\frac{4C_{7}\delta_{0}^{2}}{t})\leq\frac{C_{3}}{t^{3/2}},

(2.61)

if
\displaystyle C_{3}\geq 32C_{7}
is chosen sufficiently large and such that
\displaystyle\delta_{0}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\hat{t}}}
which gives the result. This completes the proof of Proposition
2.1
.
∎

Corollary 2.2
.

Let
\displaystyle T>\hat{t}
. Assume that the solution
\displaystyle u
of (
1.11
) satisfies on
\displaystyle[0,T]

\|u_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{2,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0},

then for all
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}},=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}

(2.62)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{i,xxx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{3}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{i,xxxx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|u_{i,xxx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{4}

Proof.

It suffices to apply Proposition (
2.1
) on
\displaystyle[t-\hat{t},t]
.
∎

Proposition 2.3
.

There exists
\displaystyle C>0
large enough depending on
\displaystyle\kappa_{A}
,
\displaystyle\kappa_{B}
,
\displaystyle\kappa
such that
if the solution
\displaystyle u
of (
1.11
) verifies:

\mbox{Tot.Var.}(u(0,\cdot))\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{C},

(2.63)

then
\displaystyle u
satisfies on the whole interval
\displaystyle[0,\hat{t}]
:

\|u_{x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{2}.

(2.64)

Proof of the proposition follows from estimates (
2.17
) with the argument as in
[
4
, Proposition 2.3]
,
[
14
, Proposition 3.6]
. We omit the proof here.

3
Gradient decomposition

We would like to decompose
\displaystyle u_{x}
in terms of travelling waves of (
1.11
). Let
\displaystyle u(t,x)=U(x-\sigma_{1}t)
be a travelling wave corresponding to 1-family. Then if satisfies

\left\{\begin{array}[]{rl}&u_{x}=v\\
&v_{x}=B^{-1}(A(u)-\sigma_{1}\mathbb{I})-B^{-1}(u)[v\cdot DB(u)]v,\\
&\sigma_{1,x}=0.\end{array}\right.

(3.1)

As it is done in Appendix
A
, we note that the gradient of 1-family travelling waves can be written under the following form

u_{x}=v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\mbox{ where }\tilde{r}_{1}=\begin{pmatrix}1\\
s(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})\end{pmatrix}

(3.2)

for some
\displaystyle C^{2}
function
\displaystyle s
. Furthermore, we can calculate that
\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{1}=r_{1}(u)+\tilde{\psi}_{2}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})r_{2}
where
\displaystyle\tilde{\psi}_{2}
is as in Appendix
A
. We note from (
2.2
) that:

s(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\tilde{\psi}_{2}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})+\frac{\frac{\partial g}{\partial u_{1}}(u)}{f^{\prime}(u_{1})-\frac{\partial g}{\partial u_{2}}(u)}.

Therefore, from (
A.17
) we deduce that

\displaystyle s(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1},\,\,s_{\sigma}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1},\,\,s_{\sigma\sigma}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1},

(3.3)

\displaystyle s_{u\sigma}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1}.

Hence, we get

\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1},\quad\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma\sigma}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1},\quad\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1}.

(3.4)

Furthermore, we can deduce some identities. Assume that
\displaystyle u(t,x)=U(x-\sigma_{1}t)
is a 1 travelling wave with speed
\displaystyle\sigma_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
since
\displaystyle U^{\prime}(x)=v_{1}(x)\tilde{r}_{1}(U(x),v_{1}(x),\sigma_{1})
and
\displaystyle u
satisfies the equation (
1.11
), we get

(v_{1}B(u)\tilde{r}_{1})_{x}=v_{1}(A(u)-\sigma_{1})\tilde{r}_{1}.

(3.5)

We observe that
\displaystyle B(u)\tilde{r}_{1}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})=\alpha_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}+s_{1}r_{2}
where
\displaystyle s_{1}=(\alpha_{2}(u)-\alpha_{1}(u_{1}))\tilde{\psi}_{2}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle(v_{1}B(u)\tilde{r}_{1})_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}s_{1}r_{2})_{x}

(3.6)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}^{2}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}(u_{1})\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}^{2}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v}

(3.7)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\quad+v_{1,x}s_{1}r_{2}+v_{1}^{2}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}+v_{1}v_{1,x}s_{1,v}r_{2}.

(3.8)

Comparing the first coordinate of (
3.5
) we get
\displaystyle\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}+v_{1}^{2}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}=(\lambda_{1}-\sigma_{1})v_{1}
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle v_{1}A(u)\tilde{r}_{1}-v_{1}^{2}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{r}_{1}-\alpha_{1}v_{1}^{2}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}-v_{1}^{2}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=v_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})\tilde{r}_{1}+v^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}v_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}v^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})s_{1,v}r_{2}.

(3.9)

We want to have a decomposition of
\displaystyle u_{x}=\begin{pmatrix}u_{1,x}\\
u_{2,x}\end{pmatrix}
in the basis
\displaystyle(\tilde{r}_{1}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1}),r_{2})
as

u_{x}(t,x)=v_{1}(t,x)\tilde{r}_{1}(u(t,x),v_{1}(t,x),\sigma_{1}(t,x))+v_{2}(t,x)r_{2}.

(3.10)

Remark 3.1
.

We point out on the fact that we will use the decomposition (
3.10
) only for
\displaystyle t\geq\hat{t}
, indeed for large time
\displaystyle t
we expect that
\displaystyle|v_{1}(t,x)=u_{1,x}(t,x)|
is sufficiently small in
\displaystyle L^{\infty}
norm such that
\displaystyle|v_{1}(t,x)|\leq\varepsilon
with
\displaystyle\varepsilon>0
defined in the Appendix. In particular we can apply the Central Manifold Theorem since
\displaystyle(u(t,x),v_{1}(t,x),\sigma_{1}(t,x))
is in a neighbourhood of
\displaystyle(u^{*},0,\lambda_{1}^{*})
.

Remark 3.2
.

We claim that the decomposition of
\displaystyle u_{x}
corresponds to a sum of gradient of viscous travelling wave. Indeed we recall that the
\displaystyle 2
Lax curve associated to (
1.1
) is of Temple type since it is a straight line. In this case (see
[
14
]
), we can observe that the gradient of the
\displaystyle 2
viscous travelling waves take the direction of
\displaystyle r_{2}
.

Remark 3.3
.

Under the previous decomposition, due to the form of the vector
\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{1}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})
(see (
3.2
)), we deduce that

v_{1}(t,x)=u_{1,x}(t,x)\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;v_{2}(t,x)=u_{2,x}(t,x)-s(u(t,x),u_{1,x}(t,x),\sigma_{1}(t,x))u_{1,x}(t,x).

(3.11)

Therefore, we can have
\displaystyle Bu_{x}=\alpha_{1}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}s_{1}r_{2}+\alpha_{2}v_{2}r_{2}
. Differentiating w.r.t.
\displaystyle x
we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle(B(u)u_{x})_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v^{2}_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+(v_{1}s_{1})_{x}r_{2}+\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}r_{2}+v_{1}^{2}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{2}^{2}(r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}.

By using (
3.9
) we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle(B(u)u_{x})_{x}-A(u)u_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v^{2}_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}^{2}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}-v_{1}A(u)\tilde{r}_{1}-v_{2}A(u)r_{2}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+v_{1,x}s_{1}r_{2}+v^{2}_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}+v_{1}v_{1,x}s_{1,v}r_{2}+v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}+\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{2}^{2}(r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1}-\lambda_{2}v_{2}r_{2}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1,x}s_{1}r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}+v_{1}v_{1,x}s_{1,v}r_{2}+v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}+\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-v_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})\tilde{r}_{1}-v^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}v_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})s_{1}r_{2}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}v^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})s_{1,v}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{2}^{2}(r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}.

We simplify

\displaystyle(B(u)u_{x})_{x}-A(u)u_{x}

(3.12)

\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})[\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}]+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})r_{2}

\displaystyle+(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-v_{2}({r}_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}))v_{2})r_{2}+v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}v_{1}\sigma_{1}s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1}s_{1,v}r_{2}+v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}.

Following
[
3
,
4
]
we set
\displaystyle w_{1}:=\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1}
. Then we have

\displaystyle(B(u)u_{x})_{x}-A(u)u_{x}

(3.13)

\displaystyle=w_{1}[\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}]

\displaystyle+(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-v_{2}({r}_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}))v_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})r_{2}+v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}+v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}.

Now, we calculate

\displaystyle\displaystyle((B(u)u_{x})_{x}-A(u)u_{x})_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})_{x}[\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}]+(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-v_{2}({r}_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}))v_{2})_{x}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+2v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\sigma_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})v_{1}[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,vv}+\sigma_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}r_{2}-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}r_{2}+2v_{1}v_{1,x}\sigma_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1}[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,vv}+\sigma_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma}]+\alpha_{1}(\sigma_{1,x}v_{1})_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+(v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma}+\sigma_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma\sigma}]+(v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x})r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}\left[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet s_{1})+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet s_{1})+v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,v}+\sigma_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,\sigma}\right]r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}(v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x})r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+(v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1})v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}\left[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})+v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\sigma_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+[v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x}](\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}((u_{x}\otimes\tilde{r}_{1}):D^{2}\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}((u_{x}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}v_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}\sigma_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}.

Similarly using (
3.13
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle u_{tx}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=v_{1,t}[\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}]+v_{2,t}r_{2}+v_{1}u_{t}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\sigma_{1,t}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=v_{1,t}[\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}]+v_{2,t}r_{2}+v_{1}\sigma_{1,t}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})v_{1}[\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{2})v_{2})v_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})v_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v^{3}_{1}\sigma_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v^{2}_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v^{2}_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v^{2}_{1}v_{2}(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v^{2}_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}).

Set
\displaystyle\tilde{\lambda}_{1},\tilde{\lambda}_{2}
as follows,

\tilde{\lambda}_{1}=\lambda_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1},\quad\tilde{\lambda}_{2}=\lambda_{2}-(r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2})v_{2}.

Since
\displaystyle u_{tx}=((B(u)u_{x})_{x}-A(u)u_{x})_{x}
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle(v_{1,t}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1})_{x}-(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x})_{x})\Big[\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}\Big]+(v_{2,t}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{2}v_{2})_{x}-(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x})_{x})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}r_{2}+(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}r_{2}-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+[w_{1}\sigma_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}(\sigma_{1,x}v_{1})_{x}-\sigma_{1,t}v_{1}+v^{2}_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}\sigma_{1,x}]\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+\mathcal{E},

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+2v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v}]
