\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}(v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x})r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+(v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1})v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}\left[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})+v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\sigma_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+[v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x}](\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}((u_{x}\otimes\tilde{r}_{1}):D^{2}\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}((u_{x}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}v_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}\sigma_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-w_{1}v_{1}^{2}\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{2})v_{2})v_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})v_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-v^{3}_{1}\sigma_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}-v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}-\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v^{2}_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-v^{2}_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}-\alpha_{1}v^{2}_{1}v_{2}(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-v^{2}_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}).

It gives then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{E}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(2v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})+\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}^{2})\tilde{r}_{1,v}+v_{1}^{2}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}-\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})\tilde{r}_{1,vv}+v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}(w_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}+v_{1}\sigma_{1})\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}^{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}-\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})+\alpha_{1}v_{1}(\sigma_{1,x})^{2}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\biggl[w_{1}v_{2}+\alpha_{1}(v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x})+(v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1})v_{1}v_{2}-v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma}-(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{2})v_{2})v_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt-\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})v_{1}-v^{2}_{1}v_{2}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})\biggl]r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(w_{1}v_{1}v_{2}+v^{2}_{1}\sigma_{1}v_{2})r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+2\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+(v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x})r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}\left[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet s_{1})+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet s_{1})+v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,v}+\sigma_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,\sigma}\right]r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}v_{1}v_{2}\left[v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})+v_{2}r_{2}\bullet(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})+v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+[v_{1,x}v_{2}+v_{1}v_{2,x}](\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}((u_{x}\otimes\tilde{r}_{1}):D^{2}\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}((u_{x}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+v_{1}v_{2}v_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}+v_{1}v_{2}\sigma_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-v^{2}_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}-\alpha_{1}v^{2}_{1}v_{2}(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}.

(3.14)

Observe that
\displaystyle\langle l_{1},\xi\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}\rangle=0
for all
\displaystyle\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
since
\displaystyle(\xi\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})_{1}=0
. Then we have
\displaystyle\langle l_{1},\mathcal{E}\rangle=0
. Hence, taking scalar product with
\displaystyle l_{1}
we obtain

v_{1,t}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1})_{x}-(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x})_{x}=0.

(3.15)

Similarly, multiplying by
\displaystyle{l}_{2}
we get

\displaystyle v_{2,t}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{2}v_{2})_{x}-(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x})_{x}

(3.16)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}+(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))

\displaystyle+[w_{1}\sigma_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}(\sigma_{1,x}v_{1})_{x}-\sigma_{1,t}v_{1}+v^{2}_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}\sigma_{1,x}](l_{2}\cdot\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma})+l_{2}\cdot\mathcal{E}.

The previous computation are satisfied for any parameter
\displaystyle\sigma_{1}
, it is then important at this level to fix our choice of
\displaystyle\sigma_{1}
. As in
[
3
]
since we consider a solution such that
\displaystyle\lim_{x\rightarrow-\infty}u(t,x)=u^{*}
, we set

\displaystyle\tilde{w}_{1}=w_{1}+\lambda_{1}(u^{*})v_{1},

(3.17)

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}=\lambda_{1}(u^{*})+\theta(-\frac{\tilde{w}_{1}}{v_{1}}),

with
\displaystyle\theta:\mathbb{R}\rightarrow[-2\delta_{1},2\delta_{1}]
an odd function such that

\theta(x)=\begin{cases}\begin{aligned} &x\;\;\;\mbox{for}&|x|\leq\delta_{1},\\
&\mbox{smooth connection}&\delta_{1}\leq|x|\leq 3\delta_{1},\\
&0&|x|\geq 3\delta_{1}.\end{aligned}\end{cases}

We take here a cubic interpolation satisfying:

|\theta(x)|\leq 2\delta_{1},\;|\theta^{\prime}(x)|\leq 1,\;|\theta^{\prime\prime}(x)|\leq\frac{1}{\delta_{1}}.

Remark 3.4
.

We can note that
\displaystyle\delta_{1}
is chosen sufficiently small such that we can define
\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{1}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})
, indeed from the Central Manifold Theorem we have seen that
\displaystyle\sigma_{1}
lives in a neighbourhood of
\displaystyle 0
.

Remark 3.5
.

The previous choice on
\displaystyle\sigma_{1}
can be explained as follows. Assume that
\displaystyle u(t,x)=U(x-\sigma t)
is a 1 travelling wave solution of (
1.11
) with
\displaystyle\sigma
close from
\displaystyle\lambda_{1}
, then from the Appendix we know that we have
\displaystyle U^{\prime}(x)=v_{1}(x)\tilde{r}_{1}(U(x),v_{1}(x),\sigma)
, in addition we have:

\displaystyle u_{t}(t,x)=-\sigma u_{x}(t,x)=-\sigma v_{1}(x-\sigma t)\tilde{r}_{1}(U(x-\sigma t),v_{1}(x-\sigma t),\sigma)
but from (
3.13
) we deduce also that:

u_{t}=w_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})r_{2}+(w_{1}+v_{1}\sigma_{1})v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}.

It is then natural to expect that
\displaystyle\sigma=-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}
when
\displaystyle\sigma
is close from
\displaystyle\lambda_{1}(u^{*})
which is exactly what expressed the truncated expression (
3.17
) when
\displaystyle|\frac{\tilde{w}_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq\delta_{1}
. In the sequel in order to simplify the notation, we will assume that
\displaystyle\lambda_{1}(u^{*})=0
such that
\displaystyle\sigma_{1}=\theta(-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}})
.

In the sequel since we will prove that the solution
\displaystyle u(t,x)
satisfies
\displaystyle\|v_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq 2\delta_{0}
for any
\displaystyle t\geq 0
and
\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}v_{1}(t,x)\|_{L^{1}}\leq 2\delta_{0}^{2}
, we choose
\displaystyle\delta_{1}
such that:

\displaystyle\|{1}/{\alpha_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}[-2\delta_{0},2\delta_{0}]}\max{(|\lambda_{1}(u(t,x))|+|\alpha^{\prime}_{1}(u_{1}(t,x))||v_{1}(t,x)|)}_{(t,x)}

\displaystyle\leq\|{1}/{\alpha_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}[-2\delta_{0},2\delta_{0}]}(2\|\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\delta_{0}+2\|\alpha^{\prime}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\delta_{0}^{2})\leq\frac{1}{5}\delta_{1}.

We deduce then that if
\displaystyle w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\neq 0
then we must have
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}
which implies in particular that

|\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}|\geq\frac{4}{5}\delta_{1}.

(3.18)

It yields that when the ratio
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|
is large, we can bound
\displaystyle|v_{1}|
by
\displaystyle|v_{1,x}|
. Similarly we can choose
\displaystyle\delta_{1}
such that when
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq\delta_{1}
then:

|\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}|\leq\frac{6}{5}\delta_{1}.

(3.19)

As previously it implies that when the ratio
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|
is small then we can control
\displaystyle|v_{1,x}|
by
\displaystyle|v_{1}|
.

We wish now to define the equation satisfied by
\displaystyle w_{1}
. Since
\displaystyle w_{1}=\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1}
we calculate

w_{1,x}=(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})_{x}=v_{1,t}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle w_{1,t}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}v_{1,tx}+u_{1,t}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1,t}-(\lambda_{1}^{\prime}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime\prime})u_{1,t}v_{1}+v_{1,t}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}w_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1})w_{1})_{x}-\alpha_{1}^{\prime}u_{1,x}w_{1,x}+u_{1,t}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1,x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\lambda_{1}^{\prime}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime\prime})u_{1,x}w_{1}-(\lambda_{1}^{\prime}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime\prime})u_{1,t}v_{1}+v_{1,t}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}-v_{1,x}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}w_{1}.

Observe that
\displaystyle u_{1,t}=w_{1}
and
\displaystyle u_{1,x}=v_{1}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\displaystyle w_{1,t}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}w_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1})w_{1})_{x}-\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}w_{1,x}+w_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1,x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\lambda_{1}^{\prime}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime\prime})v_{1}w_{1}-(\lambda_{1}^{\prime}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime\prime})w_{1}v_{1}+w_{1,x}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}-v_{1,x}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}w_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}w_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1})w_{1})_{x}.

We have obtained in particular that

w_{1,t}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{1}w_{1})_{x}-(\alpha_{1}w_{1,x})_{x}=0.

(3.20)

Let us simplify now the right hand side of (
3.16
). We denote in the sequel by
\displaystyle\theta^{(k)}_{1}
the value
\displaystyle\theta^{(k)}\left(-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)
with
\displaystyle k\in\mathbb{N}
. Now we can calculate

\displaystyle\displaystyle\alpha_{1}(\sigma_{1,x}v_{1})_{x}-\sigma_{1,t}v_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(w_{1,x}-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}v_{1,x}\right)_{x}+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}+\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(w_{1,t}-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}v_{1,t}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(w_{1,t}-\alpha_{1}w_{1,xx}-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}(v_{1,t}-\alpha_{1}v_{1,xx})\right)+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle\displaystyle w_{1,t}-\alpha_{1}w_{1,xx}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\tilde{\lambda}_{1,x}w_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}w_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}w_{1,x},

\displaystyle\displaystyle v_{1,t}-\alpha_{1}v_{1,xx}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\tilde{\lambda}_{1,x}v_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}v_{1,x}.

This implies,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left(w_{1,t}-\alpha_{1}w_{1,xx}-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}(v_{1,t}-\alpha_{1}v_{1,xx})\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}+\alpha_{1}^{\prime}(v_{1}w_{1,x}-w_{1}v_{1,x}).

It gives in particular

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}(\sigma_{1,x}v_{1})_{x}-\sigma_{1,t}v_{1}=-\theta^{\prime}_{1}\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}+\alpha_{1}^{\prime}\theta^{\prime}_{1}v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}.

It yields using the fact that
\displaystyle w_{1}=\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}

\displaystyle w_{1}\sigma_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}(\sigma_{1,x}v_{1})_{x}-\sigma_{1,t}v_{1}+\alpha^{\prime}_{1}v_{1}^{2}\sigma_{1,x}

\displaystyle=-\theta^{\prime}_{1}\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}+\alpha_{1}^{\prime}\theta^{\prime}_{1}v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1,x}+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}

\displaystyle-\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}-\alpha^{\prime}_{1}v_{1}^{2}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}.

Then we have from (
3.16
)

\displaystyle v_{2,t}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{2}v_{2})_{x}-(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x})_{x}

(3.21)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}+(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}

\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}l_{2}\cdot\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))+l_{2}\cdot\mathcal{E}.

In the sequel we will note by:

\displaystyle\phi_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}+(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}+\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}l_{2}\cdot\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

(3.22)

\displaystyle-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))+l_{2}\cdot\mathcal{E}.

4
Form of the remainder terms

In this section, we wish to prove that a solution
\displaystyle u(t,x)
of (
1.11
) satisfies for any
\displaystyle t>0

\|u_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{2,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0},

(4.1)

for
\displaystyle\delta_{0}>0
fixed and
provided that
\displaystyle\mbox{TV}(u(0,\cdot))
is sufficiently small in terms of
\displaystyle\delta_{0}
.
Let us denote by
\displaystyle\kappa_{1}
the following constant

\kappa_{1}=\max\left(1,\sup_{|u-u^{*}|\leq\varepsilon,|v_{1}|\leq\varepsilon,|\sigma_{1}|\leq 2\delta_{1}}s(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})\right),

(4.2)

with
\displaystyle\varepsilon>0
defined as in the Appendix. In addition we can take again
\displaystyle\mbox{Tot.Var.}(u(0,\cdot))
sufficiently small such that the solution
\displaystyle(u(t,x),v_{1}(t,x),\sigma_{1}(t,x))
satisfies all along the time
\displaystyle u(t,x)\in B(u^{*},\varepsilon)
and
\displaystyle v_{1}(t,x)=u_{1,x}(t,x)\in B(0,\varepsilon)
for
\displaystyle t\geq\hat{t}
.
Using the Proposition
2.3
, we know that (
4.1
) is satisfied for any
\displaystyle t\in[0,\hat{t}]
provided that
\displaystyle{Tot.Var.}(u(0,\cdot))\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{C}
with
\displaystyle C>0
sufficiently large depending on
\displaystyle\kappa,\kappa_{A}
and
\displaystyle\kappa_{B}
.

Our main goal now is to prove similar result when
\displaystyle t\geq\hat{t}
, to do this as in
[
4
]
it will require hyperbolic type estimates relying to the interactions of wave based on the previous decomposition of
\displaystyle u_{x}
as a sum of travelling wave. To do this we start by giving regularity estimates on
\displaystyle v_{1}
,
\displaystyle v_{2}
and
\displaystyle w_{1}
.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
\displaystyle T>\hat{t}
. Assume that the solution
\displaystyle u
of (
1.11
) satisfies on
\displaystyle[0,T]

\|u_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{2,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0},

then for all
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{i}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\|w_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}=O(1)\delta_{0}

(4.3)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\|w_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{i}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\|w_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\|w_{1,xx}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}+\|w_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{3},

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{i,xxx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{4}.

Proof.

It suffices to apply the Corollary
2.2
and the formula (
3.11
).
∎

We have seen that the solution
\displaystyle u
satisfies (
4.1
) for
\displaystyle t\in[0,\hat{t}]
and we know that the solution can be extended as long as its total variation remains small. Let us denote the time
\displaystyle T
satisfying,

T=\sup\left\{t,\int^{t}_{\hat{t}}|\phi_{2}(s,x)|dsdx\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{5}\right\}.

(4.4)

We want to prove that
\displaystyle T=+\infty
. We proceed then by contradiction and assume now that
\displaystyle T<+\infty
.
To do this we observe by applying maximum principle that for any
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
and the formula (
3.11
) we have

\displaystyle\|v_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle=\|u_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\|u_{1,x}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

(4.5)

\displaystyle\ \|v_{2}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\|v_{2}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\int^{t}_{\hat{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi_{2}(s,x)|dsdx,

\displaystyle\leq(\kappa_{1}\|u_{1,x}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{2,x}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}})+\frac{\delta_{0}}{5}.

It implies now that for
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
, we get using (
3.11
) and (
4.1
)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|u_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{2,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq(1+\kappa_{1})\|u_{1,x}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+(\kappa_{1}\|u_{1,x}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{2,x}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}})+\frac{\delta_{0}}{5}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\delta_{0}.

It means that we can apply Corollary
2.2
and Lemma
4.3
for any
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
and in particular we control the
\displaystyle L^{\infty}
norm of
\displaystyle v_{i,x}
,
\displaystyle v_{i,xx}
and
\displaystyle w_{1},w_{1,x}
.

We wish now to estimate
\displaystyle\int^{t}_{\hat{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi_{2}(s,x)|dsdx
and to prove that

\int^{t}_{\hat{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi_{2}(s,x)|dsdx=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}

(4.6)

which would contradict the fact that
\displaystyle T
is a supremum. From (
3.14
), we observe that each of the terms of
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}
can be estimated by the following expression

1.

wrong speed

O(1)v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}),

(4.7)

2.

change in strength

O(1)w_{1}v_{1,x}-w_{1,x}v_{1},

(4.8)

3.

change in speed

O(1)v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}\chi_{\left|w_{1}/v_{1}\right|\leq 3\delta_{1}},

(4.9)

4.

transversal interaction

O(1)v_{1}v_{2},\quad v_{1,x}v_{2},\quad v_{2,x}v_{1}.

(4.10)

For completeness we are going to estimate some of the terms of
\displaystyle\langle{\cal E},l_{2}\rangle
, essentially the more complicated. We will use in a crucial way the estimate (
3.3
) and (
3.4
). We note that some terms are completely similar to those appearing in
[
3
]
and some others one are new due to the fact that
\displaystyle\alpha_{1}
,
\displaystyle\alpha_{2}
are not constant and also because
\displaystyle s_{1}
is not necessary zero since
\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\neq\alpha_{2}
.

•

We have then using the fact that
\displaystyle w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\neq 0
only if
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}
which implies that
\displaystyle|v_{1}|=O(1)|v_{1,x}|
then it gives

v_{1}^{2}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})\langle(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}-\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}),l_{2}\rangle=O(1)v_{1}v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}).

•

Using now the fact that
\displaystyle\theta^{\prime}_{1}\neq 0
if
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq 3\delta_{1}
which implies that
\displaystyle v_{1,x}=0(1)v_{1}
we have

\displaystyle v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}(w_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}+v_{1}\sigma_{1})\langle\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma},l_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\theta^{\prime}_{1}(w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1})(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}+\alpha_{1}\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}+\sigma_{1}))\langle\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma},l_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=O(1)(w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1}).

•

The term in
\displaystyle\langle\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma\sigma},l_{2}\rangle

satisfies since
\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma\sigma}=O(1)v_{1}
,

\alpha_{1}v_{1}(\sigma_{1,x})^{2}\langle\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma\sigma},l_{2}\rangle=O(1)v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}\chi_{\left|w_{1}/v_{1}\right|\leq 3\delta_{1}}.

•

The term in
\displaystyle\langle r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma},l_{2}\rangle
verifies since
\displaystyle r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}=O(1)v_{1}
,

2\alpha_{1}\sigma_{1,x}v_{1}v_{2}\langle r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma},l_{2}\rangle=-2\alpha_{1}\theta^{\prime}_{1}v_{2}v_{1}(w_{1,x}-\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}})\langle\frac{r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}}{v_{1}},l_{2}\rangle=O(1)v_{1}v_{2}.

•

The term
\displaystyle\langle v_{1}v_{2}\sigma_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\alpha_{2})r_{2},l_{2}\rangle
can be dealt as previously.

Let us estimate now the main new terms compared with
[
3
]
on the right hand side of (
3.22
) which are
\displaystyle(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}
,
\displaystyle-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))
and
\displaystyle-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))
.
Let us start with the term
\displaystyle\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}r_{2}
. We note now that since
\displaystyle w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}
is different from
\displaystyle 0
if
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}
then we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(w_{1,x}+\sigma_{1}v_{1,x})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})\chi_{\left\{\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\leq\delta_{1}\right\}}+(w_{1,x}+\sigma_{1}v_{1,x})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1}(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v})+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})v_{1}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1,v})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})v_{2}(r_{2}\bullet s_{1}+v_{1}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,v})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})v_{1,x}(2s_{1,v}+v_{1}s_{1,vv})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})\theta^{\prime}_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}(s_{1,\sigma}+v_{1}s_{1,v\sigma})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\frac{s_{1}}{v_{1}}+s_{1,v})[(w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1})+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})v_{1,x}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v^{2}_{1}(\frac{s_{1}}{v_{1}}+s_{1,v})+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})v_{1}(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1,v})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})v_{2}(r_{2}\bullet s_{1}+v_{1}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,v})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})v_{1,x}(2s_{1,v}+v_{1}s_{1,vv})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})\theta^{\prime}_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}(s_{1,\sigma}+v_{1}s_{1,v\sigma})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}.

Since we have seen that
\displaystyle s_{1}=O(1)|v_{1}|
,
\displaystyle s_{1,\sigma}=O(1)|v_{1}|
,
\displaystyle\mbox{supp}\theta^{\prime}
is included in
\displaystyle\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq 3\delta_{1}\}
and
\displaystyle|v_{1}|=O(1)|v_{1,x}|
when
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}
we deduce that

\displaystyle\displaystyle((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1,x}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,x}\right|+\mathcal{O}(1)|v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})|

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)(|v_{1}v_{2}|+|v_{2}v_{1,x}|)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)^{2}\chi_{\left|w_{1}/v_{1}\right|\leq 3\delta_{1}}+\mathcal{O}(1)(w_{1}^{2}+v_{1}^{2})\chi_{\left\{\delta_{1}<\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\right\}}.

Let us study now the term
\displaystyle-\frac{v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}((w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})(s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}))
, which is of the form
\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(1)|v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})|
since
\displaystyle s_{1}+v_{1}s_{1,v}=\mathcal{O}(1)|v_{1}|
and
\displaystyle|v_{1}|=\mathcal{O}(1)|v_{1,x}|
when
\displaystyle w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\neq 0
.
We would like now to rewrite (
3.16
) as follows

v_{2,t}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{2}v_{2})_{x}-(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x})_{x}=\varphi_{2}+(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x},

(4.11)

where
\displaystyle\varphi_{2}
is defined as follows

\varphi_{2}=\phi_{2}-(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}.

By the analysis so far we conclude that
\displaystyle\varphi_{2}
can be estimated by (
4.7
), (
4.8
), (
4.9
) and (
4.10
). Now, we can calculate

\displaystyle\displaystyle(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-v_{1,x}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}s_{1,\sigma}+v_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}s_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\theta_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{xx}s_{1,\sigma}-\theta_{1}^{\prime}v^{2}_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1,\sigma}-\theta_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}v_{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\theta_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}v_{1,x}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}s_{1,v\sigma}+(\theta_{1}^{\prime})^{2}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}s_{1,\sigma\sigma}.

Note that

\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{xx}=\frac{w_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}w_{1}}{v_{1}^{2}}-\frac{2v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}.

(4.12)

In particular we deduce since
\displaystyle s_{1,\sigma}=\mathcal{O}(1)|v_{1}|
and because
\displaystyle\mbox{supp}\theta^{\prime}
is included in
\displaystyle\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq 3\delta_{1}\}
that

\theta_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{xx}s_{1,\sigma}=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}w_{1}\right|+\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1}\right|.

(4.13)

Since
\displaystyle s_{1,\sigma}=\mathcal{O}(1)|v_{1}|
and
\displaystyle v_{1,x}=\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1}
when
\displaystyle\theta^{\prime}_{1}\neq 0
, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle(v_{1}\sigma_{1,x}s_{1,\sigma})_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}w_{1}\right|+\mathcal{O}(1)v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}\chi_{\left\{\left|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|\leq 3\delta_{1}\right\}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1}\right|.

To summarize we have seen that all the terms on the right hand side of (
4.11
) are of the form

\displaystyle O(1)v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}),\;O(1)(w_{1}v_{1,x}-w_{1,x}v_{1}),\;\;O(1)v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}\chi_{\left|w_{1}/v_{1}\right|\leq 3\delta_{1}},

(4.14)

\displaystyle O(1)v_{1}v_{2},\;v_{1,x}v_{2},\quad v_{2,x}v_{1}\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;w_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}w_{1}.

We note that the term
\displaystyle w_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}w_{1}
does not appear in
[
3
]
. We wish now to estimate
\displaystyle L^{1}
norm of each of these terms on
\displaystyle[\hat{t},T]\times\mathbb{R}
.

5
Shortening curves

Your goal now is to deal with the new term
\displaystyle\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}w_{1}\right|
which does not appear in the analysis of
[
3
,
4
]
when we consider constant viscosity coefficients.

5.1
Scalar conservation laws

In this section we mainly discuss the application of shortening curves estimates for scalar conservation laws which reads as follows

u_{t}+f^{\prime}(u)u_{x}=(\alpha(u)u_{x})_{x}.

(5.1)

Though it has been already used in
[
3
,
4
]
to show that
\displaystyle u_{xx}u_{t}-u_{tx}u_{x}
is
\displaystyle L^{1}_{t,x}
when
\displaystyle\alpha(u)=1
, here we wish to prove that
\displaystyle u_{xxx}u_{t}-u_{txx}u_{x}
is
\displaystyle L^{1}_{t,x}
. We consider
\displaystyle v:=u_{x}
satisfying the following equation

v_{t}+(\tilde{\lambda}(u)v)_{x}=(\alpha(u)v_{x})_{x}\mbox{ where }\tilde{\lambda}(u)=f^{\prime}(u)-\alpha^{\prime}(u)v\;\;\mbox{and}\;\;\lambda(u)=f^{\prime}(u).

(5.2)

We define
\displaystyle w=\alpha v_{x}-\tilde{\lambda}v
. Observe that
\displaystyle w=u_{t}
and
\displaystyle v_{t}=w_{x}
. Then we deduce

\displaystyle\displaystyle w_{t}=(\alpha v_{x})_{t}-(\tilde{\lambda}v)_{t}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha^{\prime}(u)u_{t}v_{x}+\alpha w_{xx}-\lambda^{\prime}u_{t}v+(\alpha^{\prime}(u)v)_{t}v-\lambda v_{t}+\alpha^{\prime}(u)vv_{t}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha^{\prime}(u)wv_{x}+\alpha w_{xx}-\lambda^{\prime}wv+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)u_{t}v^{2}+\alpha^{\prime}(u)v_{t}v-\lambda v_{t}+\alpha^{\prime}(u)vv_{t}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha^{\prime}(u)wv_{x}+\alpha w_{xx}-\lambda^{\prime}wv+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)wv^{2}+\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}v-\lambda w_{x}+\alpha^{\prime}(u)vw_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha w_{x})_{x}-(\tilde{\lambda}w)_{x}.

Hence,
\displaystyle w
solves

w_{t}+(\tilde{\lambda}w)_{x}-(\alpha(u)w_{x})_{x}=0.

(5.3)

We introduce a new variable
\displaystyle z:=\alpha(u)w_{x}-\tilde{\lambda}w
. We observe that
\displaystyle z_{x}=w_{t}
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle z_{t}

\displaystyle=\alpha(u)w_{tx}+(\alpha(u))_{t}w_{x}-(\tilde{\lambda})_{t}w-\tilde{\lambda}w_{t}

(5.4)

\displaystyle=\alpha(u)z_{xx}+(\alpha(u))_{t}w_{x}-(\tilde{\lambda})_{t}w-\tilde{\lambda}z_{x}.

Let us compute now
\displaystyle-(\tilde{\lambda})_{t}w
, it gives:

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(\tilde{\lambda})_{t}w=(-\lambda^{\prime}(u)u_{t}+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)u_{u}v+\alpha^{\prime}(u)v_{t})w

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\lambda^{\prime}(u)w^{2}+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)w^{2}v+\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}w

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\lambda^{\prime}(u)w\alpha v_{x}+\lambda^{\prime}(u)w\tilde{\lambda}v+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)w\alpha v_{x}v-\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)w\tilde{\lambda}v^{2}+\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}w

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\lambda^{\prime}(u)w\alpha v_{x}+(\lambda(u))_{x}(\alpha w_{x}-z)+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)w\alpha v_{x}v-(\alpha^{\prime}(u))_{x}(\alpha w_{x}-z)v+\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}w

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-(\lambda(u))_{x}z+\lambda^{\prime}(u)\alpha(w_{x}v-v_{x}w)-(\alpha^{\prime}(u))_{x}v\alpha w_{x}+(\alpha^{\prime}(u))_{x}vz+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)w\alpha v_{x}v

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}(\alpha v_{x}-\tilde{\lambda}v)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-(\lambda(u))_{x}z+\lambda^{\prime}(u)\alpha(w_{x}v-v_{x}w)-(\alpha^{\prime}(u))_{x}v\alpha w_{x}+(\alpha^{\prime}(u))_{x}vz+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)w\alpha v_{x}v

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt-\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}\tilde{\lambda}v+\alpha^{\prime}(u)v_{x}(z+\tilde{\lambda}w)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-(\lambda(u))_{x}z+\lambda^{\prime}(u)\alpha(w_{x}v-v_{x}w)+(\alpha^{\prime}(u))_{x}vz+\alpha^{\prime}(u)v_{x}z-(\alpha^{\prime}(u))_{x}v\alpha w_{x}+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)w\alpha v_{x}v

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\alpha^{\prime}(u)v_{x}\tilde{\lambda}w-\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}\tilde{\lambda}v.

We have finally obtained:

-(\tilde{\lambda})_{t}w=-\tilde{\lambda}_{x}z+\lambda^{\prime}(u)\alpha(u)(w_{x}v-v_{x}w)+\alpha^{\prime}(u)\tilde{\lambda}(v_{x}w-w_{x}v)+\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)v\alpha(u)(wv_{x}-vw_{x}).

(5.5)

Let us estimate now
\displaystyle(\alpha(u))_{t}w_{x}
, we have then:

\displaystyle(\alpha(u))_{t}w_{x}=\alpha^{\prime}(u)ww_{x}=\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}(\alpha v_{x}-\tilde{\lambda}v)

(5.6)

\displaystyle=\alpha^{\prime}(u)v_{x}(z+\tilde{\lambda}w)-\alpha^{\prime}(u)w_{x}\tilde{\lambda}v

\displaystyle=\alpha^{\prime}(u)v_{x}z+\alpha^{\prime}(u)\tilde{\lambda}(v_{x}w-w_{x}v)

\displaystyle=(\alpha(u))_{x}z_{x}+\alpha^{\prime}(u)(v_{x}z-vz_{x})+\alpha^{\prime}(u)\tilde{\lambda}(v_{x}w-w_{x}v).

Combining (
5.4
), (
5.5
) and (
5.6
) we obtain that
\displaystyle z
solves the following equation

z_{t}+(\tilde{\lambda}z)_{x}-(\alpha(u)z_{x})=\phi,

with

\displaystyle\phi=\big(\lambda^{\prime}(u)\alpha(u)-2\alpha^{\prime}(u)\tilde{\lambda}-\alpha^{\prime\prime}(u)v\alpha(u)\big)(w_{x}v-v_{x}w)+\alpha^{\prime}(u)(v_{x}z-vz_{x}).

(5.7)

Now, let us recall the following crucial result from
[
4
]
.

Lemma 5.1
.

Let
\displaystyle\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2}
be solutions of the following equations for some
\displaystyle\varphi_{1}
and
\displaystyle\varphi_{2}
.

\displaystyle\zeta_{1,t}+(\lambda\zeta_{1})_{x}-(\alpha_{1}\zeta_{1,x})_{x}=\varphi_{1},

(5.8)

\displaystyle\zeta_{2,t}+(\lambda\zeta_{2})_{x}-(\alpha_{1}\zeta_{2,x})_{x}=\varphi_{2}.

For each
\displaystyle t
, we assume that
\displaystyle x\mapsto\zeta_{1}(t,x),\,x\mapsto\zeta_{2}(t,x)
and
\displaystyle x\mapsto\lambda(t,x)
are
\displaystyle C^{1,1}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{A}(t)\leq

\displaystyle-\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}(t,x)\left|\zeta_{1,x}(t,x)\zeta_{2}(t,x)-\zeta_{1}(t,x)\zeta_{2,x}(t,x)\right|\,dx

(5.9)

\displaystyle+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\zeta_{1}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\varphi_{2}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\zeta_{2}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\varphi_{1}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{A}
is defined as below

\mathcal{A}(t)=\frac{1}{2}\int\int\limits_{x<y}\left|\zeta_{1}(t,x)\zeta_{2}(t,y)-\zeta_{1}(t,y)\zeta_{2}(t,x)\right|\,dxdy.

(5.10)

Proof.

The proof follows the same line as in
[
4
]
except that we deal with a variable viscosity coefficient
\displaystyle\alpha_{1}
. For completeness we recall the proof. Let us set:

\gamma(t,x)=\big(\int^{x}_{-\infty}\zeta_{1}(t,y)dy,\int^{x}_{-\infty}\zeta_{2}(t,y)dy\big)

(5.11)

Integrating (
5.8
), we get:

\gamma_{t}+\lambda\gamma_{x}-\alpha_{1}\gamma_{xx}=\varphi(t,x)=\big(\int^{x}_{-\infty}\varphi_{1}(t,y)dy,\int^{x}_{-\infty}\varphi_{2}(t,y)dy\big)

(5.12)

As in
[
4
]
, for the curve
\displaystyle\gamma
, at each point where
\displaystyle\gamma_{x}\neq 0
we consider the unit vector
\displaystyle n(x)
oriented so that
\displaystyle\gamma_{x}(x)\wedge n=|\gamma_{x}(x)|>0
. In particular for every vector
\displaystyle w\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
we have

\gamma_{x}(x)\wedge v=|\gamma_{x}(x)|\;\langle n(x),v\rangle.

We define also the projection of
\displaystyle\gamma
along
\displaystyle n
by:

y\rightarrow\chi^{n}(y)=\langle n,\gamma(y)\rangle.

Following the same computations as
[
4
]
, we can show that:

\displaystyle\frac{d\mathcal{A}(t)}{dt}=\frac{1}{2}\int|\gamma_{x}(t,x)|\frac{d}{dt}(TV(\chi^{n(t,x)}))dx

where the derivative with respect to time of the total variation is considered assuming t
\displaystyle n(t,x)
is like a constant vector in time. If we assume that
\displaystyle\chi^{n(t,x)}
has a finite number of local minima and maxima
\displaystyle y_{-p},\cdots,y_{-1},y_{0}=x,y_{1},\cdots y_{q}
then we can check that:

\frac{d}{dt}(TV(\chi^{n(t,x)}))=-\mbox{sign}\langle n(t,x),\gamma_{xx}(t,x)\rangle 2\sum_{-p\leq\alpha\leq q}(-1)^{\alpha}\langle n(t,x),\gamma_{t}(t,y_{\alpha}(t))\rangle

(5.13)

Furthermore we have as in
[
4
]
:

\langle n(t,x),\gamma_{x}(t,x)\rangle=0,\;\;\mbox{sign}\langle n(t,x),\gamma_{xx}(t,y_{\alpha})\rangle=(-1)^{\alpha}\mbox{sign}\langle n(t,x),\gamma_{xx}(t,x)\rangle,

One obtain then:

\displaystyle\frac{d\mathcal{A}(t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=-\int|\gamma_{x}(t,x)|\mbox{sign}\langle n(t,x),\gamma_{xx}(t,x)\rangle\left(\sum_{-p\leq\alpha\leq q}(-1)^{\alpha}\langle n(t,x),\gamma_{t}(t,y_{\alpha}(t))\rangle\right)dx

(5.14)

\displaystyle\leq-\int\sum_{\alpha}\alpha_{1}(t,y_{\alpha}(t))|\gamma_{x}(t,x)\wedge\gamma_{xx}(t,y_{\alpha}(t))|dx

\displaystyle+\int|\gamma_{x}(t,x)||\sum_{\alpha}(-1)^{\alpha}\langle n(t,x),\varphi(t,y_{\alpha}(t))\rangle|dx

\displaystyle\leq-\int\alpha_{1}(t,x)|\gamma_{x}(t,x)\wedge\gamma_{xx}(t,x)|dx+\int\int|\gamma_{x}(t,x)\wedge\varphi_{x}(t,y)|dxdy.

This last inequality implies (
5.9
). As in
[
4
]
using an approximation argument we can assume that the function
\displaystyle\chi^{n(t,x)}
has the previous regularity.
∎

Applications:

1.

We may take
\displaystyle\zeta_{1}=v_{1}
and
\displaystyle\zeta_{2}=w_{1}
. From Lemma
5.1
we can obtain

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{A}(t)\leq-\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}(u_{1}(t,x))\left|v_{1,x}(t,\cdot)w_{1}(t,\cdot)-w_{1,x}(t,\cdot)v_{1}(t,\cdot)\right|\,dx.

Hence, we get for all
\displaystyle 0\leq T\leq T^{\prime}

\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}(u_{1})\left|v_{1,x}(t,\cdot)w_{1}(t,\cdot)-w_{1,x}(t,\cdot)v_{1}(t,\cdot)\right|\,dx\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|w_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}.

(5.15)

2.

We may take
\displaystyle\zeta_{1}=v_{1}
and
\displaystyle\zeta_{2}=z_{1}=\alpha_{1}(u_{1})w_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}w_{1}
. Define
\displaystyle\phi
as in (
5.7
), from Lemma
5.1
we can obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{A}(t)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq-\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}(u_{1}(t,x))\left|v_{1,x}(t,\cdot)z_{1}(t,\cdot)-z_{1,x}(t,\cdot)v_{1}(t,\cdot)\right|\,dx

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\phi(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}.

(5.16)

We first note that

\displaystyle\displaystyle z_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}z_{1}=(\alpha_{1}w_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}w_{1})_{x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}(\alpha_{1}w_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}w_{1})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1,x}w_{1,x}v_{1}+\alpha_{1}w_{1,xx}v_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1,x}w_{1}v_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}w_{1,x}v_{1}-\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}w_{1,x}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1,x}w_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}w_{1,xx}v_{1}-(w_{1}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1})w_{1,x}+\alpha_{1,x}w_{1,x}v_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1,x}w_{1}v_{1}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1,x}w_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}w_{1,x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}w_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}w_{1,x}+\alpha_{1,x}w_{1,x}v_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1,x}w_{1}v_{1}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1,x}w_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}w_{1,x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1})_{x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}w_{1,x}+\alpha_{1,x}w_{1,x}v_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1,x}w_{1}v_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1,x}w_{1}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}w_{1,x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}(w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,xx})+(2\tilde{\lambda}_{1}-\alpha^{\prime}_{1}(u_{1})v_{1})(w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1}).

(5.17)

From (
5.16
) we get for all
\displaystyle 0\leq T\leq T^{\prime}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}(u_{1})\left|z_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}z_{1}\right|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|z_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime}),L^{1}(\mathbb{R}))}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\phi\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}([T,T^{\prime}]\times\mathbb{R})}.

Now, we apply (
5.7
), (
5.15
), (
5.16
) and (
5.17
) to get

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}^{2}(u_{1})\left|w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,xx}\right|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}(u_{1})\left|z_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}z_{1}\right|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\|2\tilde{\lambda}_{1}-\alpha^{\prime}_{1}(u_{1})v_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}([T,T^{\prime}]\times\mathbb{R})}\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}(u_{1})\left|w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1}\right|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|z_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime});L^{1}(\mathbb{R}))}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\phi\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}([T,T^{\prime}]\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\|2\tilde{\lambda}_{1}-\alpha^{\prime}_{1}(u_{1})v_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}([T,T^{\prime}]\times\mathbb{R})}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|w_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|z_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\|2\tilde{\lambda}_{1}-\alpha^{\prime}_{1}(u_{1})v_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}([T,T^{\prime}]\times\mathbb{R})}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|w_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime});L^{1}(\mathbb{R}))}\|\lambda_{1}^{\prime}(u_{1})-2\frac{\alpha_{1}^{\prime}(u_{1})}{\alpha_{1}(u_{1})}\tilde{\lambda}-\alpha_{1}^{\prime\prime}(u_{1})v_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime})\times\mathbb{R})}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|w_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime});L^{1}(\mathbb{R}))}\|\alpha_{1}^{\prime}(u_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime})\times\mathbb{R})}\|\alpha_{1}(w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,xx})\|_{L^{1}((T,T^{\prime})\times\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime});L^{1}(\mathbb{R}))}\|\alpha_{1}^{\prime}(u_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime})\times\mathbb{R})}\times

\displaystyle\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}(u_{1})}(2\tilde{\lambda}_{1}-\alpha^{\prime}_{1}(u_{1})v_{1})\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}((T,T^{\prime})\times\mathbb{R})}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|w_{1}(T)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}.

(5.18)

We take now
\displaystyle T=\hat{t}
and
\displaystyle T^{\prime}=T
with
\displaystyle T
defined in (
4.4
). We can note in particular that

\displaystyle\left|w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,xx}\right|
and
\displaystyle|z_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}z_{1}|
are integrable on
\displaystyle[\hat{t},T]\times\mathbb{R}
due to the estimates of the Lemma
4.3
. From the Lemma
4.3
and using (
1.9
) we deduce that

\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,xx}\right|\,dxdt=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}+O(1)\delta_{0}\|\alpha_{1}(w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,xx})\|_{L^{1}((\hat{t},T)\times\mathbb{R})}.

(5.19)

We deduce then since
\displaystyle\delta_{0}>0
is chosen sufficiently small that

\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|w_{1,xx}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,xx}\right|\,dxdt=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}.

(5.20)

Similarly using (
5.15
) we have

\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|w_{1,x}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,x}\right|\,dxdt=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}.

(5.21)

5.2
Length Functional

As in
[
3
]
, we wish now to deal with the terms of the form
\displaystyle O(1)v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}\chi_{\left|w_{1}/v_{1}\right|\leq 3\delta_{1}}
. We follow the main argument of the proof of
[
4
]
except that we have variable viscosity coefficient.

Lemma 5.2
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
4.3
with
\displaystyle\zeta_{1}=v_{1}
,
\displaystyle\zeta_{2}=w_{1}
,
\displaystyle\lambda=\tilde{\lambda}_{1}
,
\displaystyle\varphi_{1}=\varphi_{2}=0
, at a fixed time
\displaystyle t
assume that
\displaystyle\gamma_{x}(t,x)\neq 0
for every
\displaystyle x
with
\displaystyle\gamma(t,x)=\big(\int^{x}_{-\infty}v_{1}(t,y)dy,\int^{x}_{-\infty}w_{1}(t,y)dy\big)
. Then

\frac{d}{dt}{\cal L}(t)\leq-\frac{1}{(1+9\delta_{1}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq 3\delta_{1}\}}|\alpha_{1}v_{1}||(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}})_{x}|^{2}dx,

(5.22)

with

{\cal L}(t)=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sqrt{v_{1}^{2}(t)+w_{1}^{2}(t)}dx.

Proof.

Let us recall that

\gamma_{t,x}+(\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\gamma_{x})_{x}-(\alpha_{1}\gamma_{xx})_{x}=0.

(5.23)

Since
\displaystyle\gamma_{x}
never vanishes, we get after integrations by parts

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\cal L}(t)=

\displaystyle\int\frac{\langle\gamma_{x},\gamma_{xt}\rangle}{\sqrt{|\gamma_{x}|^{2}}}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{\langle\gamma_{x},(\alpha_{1}\gamma_{xx})_{x}\rangle}{|\gamma_{x}|}-\frac{\langle\gamma_{x},(\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\gamma_{x})_{x}\rangle}{|\gamma_{x}|}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int(\alpha_{1}|\gamma_{x}|)_{x}-\frac{\alpha_{1}|\gamma_{xx}|^{2}}{|\gamma_{x}|}+\alpha_{1}\frac{\langle\frac{\gamma_{x}}{|\gamma_{x}|},\gamma_{xx}\rangle^{2}}{|\gamma_{x}|}-(\tilde{\lambda}_{1}|\gamma_{x}|)_{x}.

As in
[
4
]
, we have
\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\frac{\langle\frac{\gamma_{x}}{|\gamma_{x}|},\gamma_{xx}\rangle^{2}}{|\gamma_{x}|}=-\frac{\alpha_{1}|v_{1}||(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}})_{x}|^{2}}{(1+(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}})^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}
which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\cal L}(t)=-\int\frac{\alpha_{1}|v_{1}||(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}})_{x}|^{2}}{(1+(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}})^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}dx.

This last inequality implies clearly (
5.22
).
∎

From (
5.22
), (
1.9
) and applying the Lemma
4.3
, we deduce that

\int_{\hat{t}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}}v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}\chi_{\left|w_{1}/v_{1}\right|\leq 3\delta_{1}}dxds=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}.

(5.24)

6
Transversal interaction and energy estimates

In this subsection we recall the estimate for transversal interaction (see
[
4
,
14
]
)

Proposition 6.1
.

Let
\displaystyle v,v^{\#}
be solutions of the following equations

\displaystyle v_{t}+(\lambda v)_{x}-(\alpha v)_{xx}

\displaystyle=\phi,

(6.1)

\displaystyle v^{\#}_{t}+(\lambda^{\#}v^{\#})_{x}-(\alpha^{\#}v^{\#})_{xx}

\displaystyle=\phi^{\#}.

We assume that
\displaystyle\inf\lambda^{\#}-\sup\lambda\geq c_{0}>0
and that
\displaystyle\|(\alpha,\alpha^{\#})\|_{L^{\infty}}<+\infty
. Then the following estimate holds true for any
\displaystyle 0\leq T\leq T^{\prime}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|vv^{\#}\right|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{c_{1}}\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v(T,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\phi\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}([T,T^{\prime}]\times\mathbb{R})}\right)\cdot\left(\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v^{\#}(T,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R})}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\phi^{\#}\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}([T,T^{\prime}]\times\mathbb{R})}\right),

where
\displaystyle c_{1}=2c_{0}
.

Similarly we have the following proposition.

Proposition 6.2
.

Let
\displaystyle v,v^{\#}
be solutions of (
6.1
) and we assume that
\displaystyle\inf\lambda^{\#}-\sup\lambda\geq c_{0}
holds with in addition the following estimates

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\phi(t,x)\right|dxdt\leq\delta_{0},\quad\quad\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\phi^{\#}(t,x)\right|dxdt\leq\delta_{0},

(6.2)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\,\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v^{\#}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0},\quad\quad\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v_{x}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\,\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|v^{\#}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{*}\delta^{2}_{0},

(6.3)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\lambda_{x}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}},\,\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|\lambda_{x}(t)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\leq C_{*}\delta_{0},\quad\quad\lim\limits_{x\rightarrow-\infty}\lambda(t,x)=0,

(6.4)

for all
\displaystyle t\in[T,T^{\prime}]
. Then we have

\int\limits_{0}^{T}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|v_{x}(t,x)\right|\left|v^{\#}(t,x)\right|\,dxdt=\mathcal{O}(1)\delta^{2}_{0}.

(6.5)

We can apply directly the Proposition
6.1
to
\displaystyle v=v_{1}
and
\displaystyle v^{\#}=v_{2}
. We have then
\displaystyle\lambda=\lambda_{1}
,
\displaystyle\phi=0
,
\displaystyle\lambda^{\#}=\lambda_{2}-(r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2})v_{2}+u_{x}\bullet\alpha_{2}=\lambda_{2}+(r_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2})v_{1}
,
\displaystyle\phi^{\#}=\varphi_{2}
. We can observe that using (
1.6
), the Lemma
4.3
and taking
\displaystyle\delta_{0}
sufficiently small we deduce that
\displaystyle\inf\lambda^{\#}-\sup\lambda\geq c_{0}>0
with
\displaystyle c_{0}=\frac{c}{2}
. It gives by applying again Lemma
4.3
that

\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|v_{1}v_{2}\right|\,dxdt=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}.

(6.6)

Similarly by applying the Proposition
6.5
we can show that

\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|v_{1}v_{2,x}\right|\,dxdt=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2},\;\;\int\limits_{T}^{T^{\prime}}\int\limits_{\mathbb{R}}\left|v_{1,x}v_{2}\right|\,dxdt=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}.

(6.7)

As in
[
4
]
, we are going to prove energy estimate. Define now a cut-off positive function
\displaystyle\hat{\theta}
satisfying

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}(x)=\begin{cases}\begin{aligned} &0\;\;\;|x|\leq\frac{3\delta_{1}}{5},\\
&\mbox{smooth connection}\;\;\ \frac{3\delta_{1}}{5}\leq|x|\leq\frac{4\delta_{1}}{5},\\
&1\;\;\;|x|\geq\frac{4\delta_{1}}{5}.\end{aligned}\end{cases}

(6.8)

We assume here that
\displaystyle\delta_{1}|\hat{\theta}^{\prime}|,\delta_{1}^{2}|\hat{\theta}^{\prime\prime}|\leq 16
.
Let us multiply now (
3.15
) by
\displaystyle v_{1}\theta\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)
and integrate by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\biggl((\frac{v_{1}^{2}\hat{\theta}}{2})_{t}-\frac{v_{1}^{2}}{2}(\hat{\theta}_{t}+2\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}-(\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x})_{x})+2\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}v_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}+\hat{\theta}v_{1,x}(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1})\biggl)dx=0.

It implies that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hat{\theta}v_{1,x}(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1})=

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\frac{v_{1}^{2}\hat{\theta}}{2})_{t}dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{v_{1}^{2}}{2}(\hat{\theta}_{t}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}-(\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x})_{x})dx

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}}2\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}v_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{v_{1}^{2}}{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}.

(6.9)

A direct computation gives using (
4.12
),

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}_{t}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}-(\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x})_{x}

\displaystyle=\hat{\theta}^{\prime}(\frac{w_{1,t}}{v_{1}}-\frac{v_{1,t}w_{1}}{v_{1}^{2}})+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}(\frac{w_{1,x}}{v_{1}}-\frac{v_{1,x}w_{1}}{v_{1}^{2}})

\displaystyle-\alpha^{\prime}_{1}v_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}-\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}+2\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime}\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}-\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime}(\frac{w_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}w_{1}}{v_{1}^{2}})

\displaystyle=\frac{\hat{\theta}^{\prime}}{v_{1}}(w_{1,t}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}w_{1,x}-(\alpha_{1}w_{1,x})_{x})-\frac{\hat{\theta}^{\prime}w_{1}}{v_{1}^{2}}(v_{1,t}+\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1,x}-(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x})_{x})-\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime}\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}

\displaystyle=-\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}+2\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}.

Putting this last expression in (
6.9
), it yields

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hat{\theta}v_{1,x}(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1})

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\frac{v_{1}^{2}\hat{\theta}}{2})_{t}dx-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}\hat{\theta}^{\prime\prime}v_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}dx

(6.10)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}v_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{v_{1}^{2}}{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\hat{\theta}_{x}.

We observe that
\displaystyle\hat{\theta}(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}})\neq 0
if

\displaystyle|w_{1}|=|\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-\tilde{\lambda}_{1}v_{1}|\geq\frac{3\delta_{1}}{5}|v_{1}|
which gives

|\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}|\geq\frac{3\delta_{1}}{5}|v_{1}|-\|\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}|v_{1}|\geq 2\|\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}|v_{1}|,

(6.11)

if we assume that
\displaystyle\delta_{1}
satisfies:

\delta_{1}\geq 10\|\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\delta_{0}+2\delta_{0}\|\alpha^{\prime}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\geq 5\|\tilde{\lambda}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

(6.12)

From (
6.10
), (
6.11
), (
1.9
) we get for
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]

\displaystyle\frac{c_{1}}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hat{\theta}v_{1,x}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\frac{v_{1}^{2}\hat{\theta}}{2}\right)_{t}dx+O(1)\int_{\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq\delta_{1}\}}v_{1}^{2}|\hat{\theta}^{\prime\prime}|\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}dx

(6.13)

\displaystyle+O(1)\int_{\mathbb{R}}(1+|\frac{v_{1,x}}{v_{1}}|\chi_{\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq\frac{4\delta_{1}}{5}\}})|w_{1,x}v_{1}-v_{1,x}w_{1}|dx.

We can now estimate the term
\displaystyle v_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})
, using the fact that this term is different from zero only if
\displaystyle|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}
we get from (
6.10
), (
5.24
), (
5.21
) and Lemma
4.3

\displaystyle\int^{T}_{\hat{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|v_{1,x}(w_{1}-\sigma_{1}v_{1})|dxds

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\int^{T}_{\hat{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi_{\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}\}}(v_{1,x}^{2}+|v_{1}v_{1,x}|)dsdx

(6.14)

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\int^{T}_{\hat{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\chi_{\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}\}}v_{1,x}^{2}dsdx\leq O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}.

Finally using (
4.14
), (
5.20
), (
5.21
), (
5.24
), (
6.6
), (
6.7
) and (
6.14
) we have proved that

\int_{\hat{t}}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\phi_{2}(s,x)|dsdx\leq O(1)\delta_{0}^{2},

which contradict the definition of
\displaystyle T
provided that
\displaystyle\delta_{0}
is chosen sufficiently small. In particular since
\displaystyle T=+\infty
we deduce by maximum principle that we have for any
\displaystyle t\in\mathbb{R}

\|u_{1,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{2,x}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0}.

(6.15)

7
Stability

We wish now to get stability estimate, in other words let us consider two initial data
\displaystyle\bar{u}
and
\displaystyle\bar{v}
of the equation (
1.11
) satisfying the smallness assumption on
\displaystyle\mbox{Tot.Var.}\bar{u}
and
\displaystyle\mbox{Tot.Var.}\bar{v}
such that (
1.11
) admits respectively a global solution
\displaystyle u
and
\displaystyle v
associated to the initial data
\displaystyle\bar{u}
and
\displaystyle\bar{v}
with small Total Variation. In addition we assume that
\displaystyle(\bar{u}-\bar{v})\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R})
then we are going to prove that there exists a constant
\displaystyle L
depending on
\displaystyle\delta_{0}
such that for any
\displaystyle t\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
we have

\|u(t)-v(t)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\|\bar{u}-\bar{v}\|_{L^{1}}.

(7.1)

To do this following
[
4
]
, we employ an homotopy argument, setting
\displaystyle\bar{u}^{\theta}=\theta\bar{u}+(1-\theta)\bar{v}
with
\displaystyle\theta\in[0,1]
and denoting by
\displaystyle u^{\theta}
the solution of
(
1.11
) with this initial data (this solution exists due to the Theorem
1.1
) we are going to consider the unknown
\displaystyle z^{\theta}(t,x)=\frac{du^{\theta}}{d\theta}(t,x)
which satisfies the following system

\begin{cases}\begin{aligned} &z^{\theta}_{t}+(A(u)z^{\theta})_{x}=\left(B(u)z^{\theta}_{x}+z^{\theta}\bullet B(u^{\theta})u^{\theta}_{x}\right)_{x},\\
&z^{\theta}(0,\cdot)=\bar{u}-\bar{v}.\end{aligned}\end{cases}

(7.2)

Indeed we can observe that
\displaystyle u^{\theta}_{x}\bullet A(u^{\theta})z^{\theta}=z^{\theta}\bullet A(u^{\theta})u^{\theta}_{x}
. It remains now to estimate the
\displaystyle L^{1}
norm of
\displaystyle z^{\theta}
all along the time, in particular if we show that for any
\displaystyle t\geq 0

\|z^{\theta}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq L\|z^{\theta}(0,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}},

(7.3)

then we deduce that for any
\displaystyle t\geq 0

\displaystyle\|u(t)-v(t)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\int^{1}_{0}\|z^{\theta}(s)\|ds\leq L\|\bar{u}-\bar{v}\|_{L^{1}},

which is exactly the estimate (
7.1
) what conclude the proof of the stability. It remains now to prove the estimate (
7.3
). We are reduced to study the
\displaystyle L^{1}
norm of the solution of the following equation with
\displaystyle u
satisfying (
1.11
)

h_{t}+(Ah)_{x}=(Bh)_{xx}+(h\bullet Bu_{x}-u_{x}\bullet Bh)_{x}.

(7.4)

7.1
Parabolic estimates

As in the previous section, we can get regularity estimates on
\displaystyle h
, by applying similar arguments (it is clear since
\displaystyle h
satisfies the same equation than
\displaystyle u_{x}
except that the initial data is a priori different).

Proposition 7.1
.

There exists
\displaystyle C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}>0
depending on
\displaystyle\kappa_{A},\kappa_{B},\kappa
such that if
\displaystyle h
is the solution of the equation (
7.4
) satisfying

\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|(h)_{1}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}+\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|(h)_{2}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0}\mbox{ for all }t\in[0,\hat{t}]\mbox{ where }\hat{t}\leq\frac{1}{(C_{3}\delta_{0})^{2}},

(7.5)

for
\displaystyle C_{3}
sufficiently large depending on
\displaystyle\kappa_{A},\kappa_{B},\kappa
and
\displaystyle\delta_{0}
sufficiently small in terms of
\displaystyle C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}
,
\displaystyle\kappa_{A}
,
\displaystyle\kappa_{B}
and
\displaystyle\kappa
then
we have for
\displaystyle i\in\{1,2\}

\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{C_{1}\delta_{0}}{\sqrt{t}},\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{C_{2}\delta_{0}}{t},\mbox{ and }\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xxx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{C_{3}}{t^{3/2}}.

(7.6)

Corollary 7.2
.

Let
\displaystyle T>\hat{t}
. Assume that the solution
\displaystyle h
of (
7.4
) satisfies on
\displaystyle[0,T]

\|(h)_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|(h)_{2}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0},

then for all
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}},=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2},

(7.7)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{3},

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xxx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{4}.

Proof.

It suffices to apply Proposition (
2.1
) on
\displaystyle[t-\hat{t},t]
.
∎

Proposition 7.3
.

There exists
\displaystyle C>0
large enough depending on
\displaystyle\kappa_{A}
,
\displaystyle\kappa_{B}
,
\displaystyle\kappa
such that
if the solution
\displaystyle h
of (
7.4
) verifies:

\|h(0,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{C},

(7.8)

then
\displaystyle h
satisfies on the whole interval
\displaystyle[0,\hat{t}]
:

\|h(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{2}.

(7.9)

7.2
Gradient decomposition

We wish now to estimate on
\displaystyle h(t,\cdot)
in
\displaystyle L^{1}
when
\displaystyle t\geq\hat{t}
. As previously we decompose

\displaystyle h
as a sum of viscous travelling waves

h=h_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}(u,v_{1},\sigma_{1})+h_{2}r_{2},

(7.10)

with the same choice on
\displaystyle\sigma_{1}
as in (
3.17
). In the sequel we distinguish
\displaystyle(h)_{2}
which is the second coordinate of
\displaystyle h
whereas
\displaystyle h_{2}
is the new variable introduced in (
7.10
). From the previous estimate on
\displaystyle h
we deduce that

Lemma 7.4
.

Let
\displaystyle T>\hat{t}
. Assume that the solution
\displaystyle h
of (
7.4
) satisfies on
\displaystyle[0,T]

\|(h)_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|(h)_{2}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\delta_{0},

then for all
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}}=O(1)\delta_{0}

(7.11)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,x}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{3},

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xxx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{1}},\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left|\hskip-0.56905pt\left|h_{i,xx}(t,\cdot)\right|\hskip-0.56905pt\right|_{L^{\infty}}=O(1)\delta_{0}^{4}.

We calculate

\displaystyle\displaystyle(Bh)_{x}+(h\bullet Bu_{x}-u_{x}\bullet Bh)-Ah

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}h_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}+(s_{1}h_{1}+\alpha_{2}h_{2})r_{2})_{x}-h_{1}A\tilde{r}_{1}-\lambda_{2}h_{2}r_{2}+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})(\tilde{r}_{1}\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB\tilde{r}_{1})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1}+h_{1}\left[-A\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}\right]+\alpha_{1}v_{2}h_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}h_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+\alpha_{1}h_{1}\sigma_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+h_{1,x}s_{1}r_{2}+v_{2}h_{1}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}h_{1}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}+v_{1,x}h_{1}s_{1,v}r_{2}+\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{1}h_{2}r_{2}+r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{2}h_{2}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{2}h_{2,x}-\lambda_{2}h_{2})r_{2}+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})(\tilde{r}_{1}\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB\tilde{r}_{1}).

By using (
3.9
) we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle(Bh)_{x}-Ah

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})\tilde{r}_{1}+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})h_{1})s_{1}r_{2}+\alpha_{1}v_{2}h_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}s_{1,v}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\alpha_{1}h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+v_{2}h_{1}r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}-h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{1}h_{2}r_{2}+(\alpha_{2}h_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{2})h_{2})r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})(\tilde{r}_{1}\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB\tilde{r}_{1})

Taking derivative with respect to
\displaystyle x
we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle[(Bh)_{x}+(h\bullet Bu_{x}-u_{x}\bullet Bh)-Ah]_{x}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})_{x}\tilde{r}_{1}+(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})_{x}h_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,vv}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})h_{1})s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})h_{1})s_{1})_{x}r_{2}+\alpha^{\prime}_{1}v_{1}v_{2}h_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}(v_{2,x}h_{1}+v_{2}h_{1,x})r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}v_{2}h_{1}(r_{2}\otimes u_{x}):D^{2}_{u}\tilde{r}_{1}+\alpha_{1}v_{2}h_{1}v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}-\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{2}h_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}}{\alpha^{2}_{1}}(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}s_{1,v}r_{2}+\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}((\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})v_{1})h_{1}s_{1,v})_{x}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}-\alpha_{1}h_{1}\left[-\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}+\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{xx}\right]\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}-\alpha_{1}h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}-\alpha_{1}h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\alpha_{1}h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma}+\alpha_{1}h_{1}(\theta_{1}^{\prime})^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)^{2}_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(v_{2,x}h_{1}+v_{2}h_{1,x})r_{2}\bullet s_{1}r_{2}+v_{2}h_{1}(u_{x}\otimes r_{2}):D^{2}s_{1}r_{2}+v_{2}h_{1}v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,v}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-v_{2}h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}+h_{1}\left[\theta_{1}^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)^{2}_{x}-\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{xx}\right]s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}-h_{1,x}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}-h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet s_{1,\sigma}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+h_{1}(\theta_{1}^{\prime})^{2}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)^{2}_{x}s_{1,\sigma\sigma}r_{2}-h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1,x}s_{1,v\sigma}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(\tilde{r}_{1}\otimes u_{x}):D^{2}\alpha_{2}v_{1}h_{2}r_{2}+(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\alpha_{2}v^{2}_{1}h_{2}r_{2}+(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{1}v_{2}h_{2}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{1}v_{1,x}h_{2}r_{2}-\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\bullet\alpha_{2}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{1}h_{2}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\alpha_{2}(v_{1,x}h_{2}+v_{1}h_{2,x})r_{2}+(\alpha_{2}h_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{2})h_{2})_{x}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})_{x}(\tilde{r}_{1}\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB\tilde{r}_{1})+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})v_{1}((\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB(\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}))

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})v_{2}((r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1})\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB(r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}))+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})v_{1,x}(\tilde{r}_{1,v}\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB\tilde{r}_{1,v})

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}(\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}\cdot DBr_{2}-r_{2}\cdot DB\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma})

\displaystyle\displaystyle+(h_{1}v_{2}-h_{2}v_{1})((\tilde{r}_{1}\otimes u_{x})\cdot D^{2}Br_{2}-(u_{x}\otimes r_{2})\cdot D^{2}B\tilde{r}_{1}).

Similarly, we have using (
3.12
)

\displaystyle\displaystyle h_{t}

Lemma 7.6
.

The remainder terms in
\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}
satisfies

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}w_{1}-h_{1}w_{1,x}\right|+\left|w_{1,x}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,x}\right|\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|h_{1}v_{2}\right|+\left|h_{1}v_{2,x}\right|+\left|h_{2}v_{1}\right|+\left|h_{2}v_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{2,x}v_{1}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}v_{2}\right|\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)(\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1}\right|)\left|v_{1}\right|\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}\chi_{\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq 3\delta_{1}\}}+\mathcal{O}(1)\left|h_{1,xx}v_{1}-v_{1,xx}h_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\right|(\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|v_{1}\right|+|v_{1,x}|)+\mathcal{O}(1)\left|h_{1,xx}w_{1}-w_{1,xx}h_{1}\right|.

Proof of Lemma
7.6
:

We observe that

\displaystyle\displaystyle(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}-(\alpha_{1}v_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})v_{1})h_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}(h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x})\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}=O(1)|h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x}|.

Then we note that using (
7.16
)

\displaystyle\displaystyle(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+[(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})v_{1,x}+(\sigma_{1}v_{1})_{x}h_{1}]\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}+[h_{1,x}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})+\sigma_{1}(v_{1,x}h_{1}-h_{1,x}v_{1})+(\sigma_{1})_{x}v_{1}h_{1}]\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\big(\left|w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\right|\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}w_{1}-h_{1}w_{1,x}\right|+\left|v_{2}\right|(\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1}\right|)\big).

Since
\displaystyle\left|\theta_{1}^{\prime}v_{1,x}\right|=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|v_{1}\right|
,
\displaystyle\left|\theta_{1}^{\prime}\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right|=\mathcal{O}(1)
,

\displaystyle b\leq 1+b^{2}
we get

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\theta_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime})h_{1})[\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}/v_{1}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\theta_{1}^{\prime}\right|\left[\left|h_{1,x}v_{1}\right|\left|\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\right|+\left|h_{1}\left(w_{1,x}-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}v_{1,x}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\theta_{1}^{\prime}\right|\left[\left|h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x}\right|\left|\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\right|+\left|-\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}(h_{1}v_{1,x}-v_{1}h_{1,x})+(h_{1}w_{1,x}-w_{1}h_{1,x})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|\theta_{1}^{\prime}\right|\left[\left|h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x}\right|+(\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1}\right|)\left|v_{1}\right|\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}+\left|h_{1,x}w_{1}-h_{1}w_{1,x}\right|\right].

We can check

\displaystyle\displaystyle(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})h_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,v}+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})h_{1}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\right|(\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|v_{1}\right|),

\displaystyle\displaystyle(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})h_{1}v_{2}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}+(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})h_{1}v_{1,x}\tilde{r}_{1,vv}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\big(\left|w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\right|\left|v_{1,x}\right|+\left|v_{1,x}v_{2}\right|+\left|v_{1}v_{2}\right|\big).

Using (

) and the fact that

we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,v\sigma}

We note now that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}}{\alpha_{1}^{2}}(\alpha_{1}h_{1,x}-(\lambda_{1}-v_{1}\alpha_{1}^{\prime}-\sigma_{1})h_{1})s_{1}r_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\alpha_{1}v_{2}h_{1}v_{1,x}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,v}-\alpha_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}v_{2}h_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)(\left|h_{1}v_{2}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}v_{2}\right|+\left|h_{1}v_{2,x}\right|)

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{\prime}v_{1}}{\alpha^{2}_{1}}(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})h_{1}s_{1,v}r_{2}-v_{1}\alpha^{\prime}_{1}h_{1}\theta_{1}^{\prime}\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}\tilde{r}_{1,\sigma}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\big(\left|w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\right|\left|v_{1}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}w_{1}-h_{1}w_{1,x}\right|\big).

Similarly we have

,

\displaystyle s_{1}=O(1)v_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-(w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1})h_{1}v_{1}\tilde{r}_{1,v}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}-(\alpha_{2}v_{2,x}-(\lambda_{2}-r_{2}\bullet\alpha_{2}v_{2})v_{2})h_{1}r_{2}\bullet\tilde{r}_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\left|h_{1}v_{2}\right|.

This completes the proof of Lemma

.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\big(\left|h_{1,xx}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,xx}\right|+\left|h_{1,xx}w_{1}-h_{1}w_{1,xx}\right|+\left|w_{1}+\sigma_{1}v_{1}\right|(\left|h_{1}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|v_{1}\right|+\left|v_{1,x}\right|)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\left|w_{1,x}v_{1}-w_{1}v_{1,x}\right|\big)+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\chi_{\{|\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}|\leq 3\delta_{1}\}}(\left|h_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{1}\right|)\left|v_{1}\right|\left(\frac{w_{1}}{v_{1}}\right)_{x}^{2}+\left|h_{1,x}v_{1}-h_{1}v_{1,x}\right|

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\left|h_{1,x}w_{1}-h_{1}w_{1,x}\right|\Big)+\mathcal{O}(1)\Big(\left|h_{1}v_{2}\right|+\left|h_{1}v_{2,x}\right|+\left|h_{2}v_{1}\right|+\left|h_{2}v_{1,x}\right|+\left|h_{2,x}v_{1}\right|+\left|h_{1,x}v_{2}\right|\Big)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}(1)|h_{1,x}|^{2}\chi_{\{|\frac{\hat{h}_{1}}{h_{1}}|\geq\delta_{1}\}}.

(7.20)

We have seen that taking the initial data
\displaystyle h(0,\cdot)
sufficiently small in
\displaystyle L^{1}
we can assume that

\|(h)_{1}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|(h)_{2}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{5\kappa_{1}}.

(7.21)

In addition the solution
\displaystyle h
of (

) for

and we know that the solution can be extended as long as the

norm of
\displaystyle h
remains small. Let us denote the time
\displaystyle T
satisfying:

T=\sup\{t,\int^{t}_{\hat{t}}|\tilde{\varphi}_{2}(s,x)|dsdx\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{5}\}.

(7.22)

We want to prove that
\displaystyle T=+\infty
. We proceed again by contradiction and assume now that
\displaystyle T<+\infty
.
Since
\displaystyle h_{1}
and
\displaystyle h_{2}
satisfy for

respectively the equations (

), applying maximum principle we deduce that for any

we have

\displaystyle\|h_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\|(h)_{1}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

(7.23)

\displaystyle\ \|h_{2}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\|h_{2}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\int^{t}_{\hat{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\tilde{\varphi}_{2}(s,x)|dxds

\displaystyle\leq(\kappa_{1}\|h_{1}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|(h)_{2}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}})+\frac{\delta_{0}}{5}.

It implies now that for
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
, we get using (
7.21
)

\|(h)_{1}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|(h)_{2}(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}\leq(1+2\kappa_{1})\|(h)_{1}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\|(h)_{2}(\hat{t},\cdot)\|_{L^{1}}+\frac{\delta_{0}}{5}\leq\delta_{0}.

(7.24)

It means that we can apply the Corollary
7.2
and the Lemma

for any
\displaystyle t\in[\hat{t},T]
and in particular we control the
\displaystyle L^{\infty}
norm of
\displaystyle h_{i}
,
\displaystyle h_{i,x}
and
\displaystyle\hat{h}_{1},\hat{h}_{1,x}
.

We wish now to estimate

which would contradict the fact that

is a supremum. To do this, we must estimate each terms of (

). Using (

) and (

), we have

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\delta_{0}^{2},

(7.26)

Using now the Lemma

, Lemma

and (

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1)\delta_{0}^{2}.

(7.27)

Since
\displaystyle\tilde{\varphi}_{2}
satisfies (

), applying Propositions

and

we obtain that
